Original Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

中文摘要翻译: 推理上下文长度与RL后训练之间的差距正日益扩大：推理系统已接近百万token级别的上下文，而后训练工作负载通常仍停留在256K tokens或以下，并依赖于部署时的长度泛化。这一差距对AI智能体尤为重要，因为其观测结果、工具输出、文档以及先前的决策会在长轨迹中不断累积。LongStraw是一个架构感知的执行栈，用于在固定GPU预算下进行百万token级别的RL后训练，并以分组相对策略优化（GRPO）作为实例化方法。它评估共享提示时不计算autograd梯度，仅保留后续token所需的模型特定状态，并逐一重放短响应分支，从而以减少实时训练图为代价换取额外的重放时间。我们针对混合循环与全注意力架构的Qwen3.6-27B以及压缩注意力混合专家架构的GLM-5.2实现了该方法。在八块H20 GPU上，LongStraw完成了分组大小为2和8的分组Qwen评分与响应反向传播，覆盖210万位置；增加分组大小仅增加0.21 GB的峰值分配内存，而独立的压力测试达到了446万位置。在32块H20 GPU上，我们验证了端到端LongStraw执行路径，在GLM-5.2的全部78层上处理了210万token的提示。这些实验建立的是执行容量而非完整的训练正确性，因为捕获的提示状态是分离的，且部分分布式前向与梯度组合路径仍不完备。

</details>


### [176] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多轴max@K，一种基于强化学习的组级目标函数，用于提升扩散文生图模型在目标模式上的覆盖多样性，并缓解人口统计偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的文生图(T2I)模型虽能生成高质量图像，但对同一提示词生成的样本往往只覆盖少数视觉上不同的模式，多样性不足；在涉及人物的提示词上，这种偏差可能反映或放大人口统计偏见。

Method: 提出多轴max@K方法：给定一组样本和每个目标类别的一个分数，先对每个类别取样本间最大值，再求和这些类别级最大值。信用分配机制确保样本仅在增加其类别的组级最大值时才获得正向权重，从而让不同样本贡献于不同类别。

Result: 在合成混合数据和SD3.5-M上使用确定性像素级颜色奖励验证了信用分配机制；在感知外观公平性评估中，多轴max@K在三个自动评估器上将公平性分数提升0.23-0.36，同时保持图像质量和文本对齐。

Conclusion: 多轴max@K能有效提升扩散文生图模型的目标模式覆盖率和人口统计公平性，且不牺牲图像质量与文本对齐。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像（T2I）模型能够合成逼真的、与提示词对齐的图像，然而为同一提示词生成的样本通常仅覆盖少数视觉上不同的模式。这限制了图像的多样性，并且对于以人物为中心的提示词，可能反映或放大人口统计偏差。我们将此问题形式化为对预定义的语义指定模式集合的覆盖，称之为目标模式覆盖。随后我们提出多轴max@K，一种基于组的强化学习目标函数，用于改进扩散式T2I模型中的此类覆盖。给定一组样本和每个目标类别的一个分数，多轴max@K首先对每个类别取样本间最大值，然后对这些类别级最大值求和。由此产生的信用分配机制使得样本仅当其增加该类别的组级最大值时才获得该类别上的正向权重，从而允许不同样本贡献于不同类别。我们首先在合成混合数据上以及在SD3.5-M上使用确定性像素级颜色奖励验证了该信用分配机制。随后，我们在感知外观公平性上评估了同一目标函数。在三个自动评估器对保留提示词的评估中，多轴max@K相比基模型将公平性分数提升了0.23-0.36，同时保持了图像质量和文本对齐。

</details>


### [177] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态语义感知对比学习（MseaCL），通过利用放射学报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，缓解医学多模态对比学习中的假阴性样本问题，在儿科脑肿瘤分子分类任务中实现AUC提升至少22.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习假设批次内非配对样本均为负样本，但在医学场景中，不同样本可能共享高级语义属性（如同类疾病），导致假阴性样本退化表征质量。需要一种能感知语义相似性的对比学习框架来缓解该问题。

Method: 提出MseaCL框架，在儿科3D脑MRI和放射学报告的多模态数据上训练。核心创新是将放射学报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号融入对比学习过程，在拉近匹配正样本的同时，避免将语义相似的非配对样本错误地视为负样本。

Result: 作为预训练阶段，MseaCL在下游任务上取得显著提升：儿科脑肿瘤分子分类的AUC至少提高22.6%，证明了其生成更鲁棒、语义对齐的多模态表征的能力。

Conclusion: MseaCL通过引入语义相似性引导信号，有效缓解了医学多模态对比学习中的假阴性问题，生成的语义对齐表征在临床下游任务中表现出显著优势，具有临床应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态对比学习（CL）在跨多种数据模态对齐表征以及改进下游任务方面展现出了显著性能，尤其在医疗保健领域。其工作原理是最小化匹配（正样本）数据模态之间的距离，同时最大化不匹配（负样本）之间的距离。传统的对比学习框架通常假设数据批次内存在基于实例的对应关系，将所有非配对样本视为负样本。然而，这一假设在医学场景中常常失效——样本可能共享高层级语义属性，从而导致假阴性样本的出现，进而退化了表征质量。在本文中，我们提出了多模态语义感知对比学习（MseaCL），这是一个在儿科3D脑磁共振成像（MRI）扫描与放射学报告队列上训练的对比学习框架。该框架的目标是通过在训练过程中引入放射学报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，减轻语义相似的假阴性样本带来的影响。我们的结果表明，将该框架作为预训练阶段应用，可以在下游任务中实现显著提升，例如儿科脑肿瘤分子分类的受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUC）至少提高了22.6%，展示了其在临床应用中生成更鲁棒且语义对齐的多模态表征的潜力。

</details>


### [178] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kernel-WIS的新型估计器，用于基于离线数据的上下文赌博机离线策略评估。该估计器结合了加权重要性采样的有界性和普通重要性采样的线性特性，在理论上具有渐近一致性，并在复杂条件下（包括行为策略错误指定）经验性地优于强基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法如普通重要性采样（IS）方差大，而加权重要性采样（WIS）虽方差有界但在某些复杂条件下表现不佳，尤其是在行为策略存在错误指定时。作者希望结合两者的优势来改进离线策略评估的性能。

Method: 提出Kernel-WIS估计器，将加权重要性采样的有界性与普通重要性采样的线性特性相结合，利用核函数进行平滑处理，实现在线策略价值函数的无偏或低偏估计。

Result: 理论分析证明所提估计器具有渐近一致性。实验结果表明，在包括行为策略错误指定在内的复杂条件下，Kernel-WIS在经验上显著优于包括普通加权重要性采样在内的强基线方法。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS通过融合有界性和线性两种特性，为上下文赌博机离线策略评估提供了一种更稳健、更有效的估计方案。

Original Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种新颖的估计器，用于仅利用离线数据进行上下文赌博机场景下的离线策略评估。所提出的Kernel-WIS估计器被证明具有渐近一致性，并且在经验上优于强基线方法（包括普通加权重要性采样），尤其在行为策略错误指定等复杂条件下表现突出。Kernel-WIS的优势在于它结合了普通加权重要性采样的有界性与普通重要性采样的线性特性。

</details>


### [179] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇SINDy教程，针对工程应用中数据稀缺且需求可解释性的场景，介绍了从标准SINDy到噪声鲁棒、集成、约束及参数化等扩展变体，并通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个案例展示其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工程问题中控制方程难以精确获知，神经网络等代理建模方法需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性，而SINDy方法能从小数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，但其在实际工程问题中的应用文档较少。

Method: 基于稀疏回归的SINDy方法，在候选非线性项库上进行稀疏回归以识别动力学方程。教程逐步介绍标准SINDy算法及其扩展变体，包括噪声鲁棒的弱形式、集成方法、约束形式和参数化形式。

Result: 通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个详细案例研究，展示了SINDy方法在工程应用中的有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: SINDy方法实现简单且足够灵活，可作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具。

Original Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

中文摘要翻译: 许多工程问题涉及的现象，其控制方程难以精确刻画或仅部分已知。神经网络等代理建模技术可以捕捉这些系统的行为，但它们通常需要大量训练数据——这在工程环境下难以获取——且生成的模型物理可解释性有限。稀疏非线性动力学辨识（SINDy）方法通过在线索性项库上进行稀疏回归，从相对较小的数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，从而同时解决了上述两个局限性。尽管SINDy已在典型基准系统上得到广泛验证，但其在实际工程问题中的应用报道较少。本教程介绍了SINDy方法，并逐步构建其主要的扩展形式，从噪声鲁棒的弱形式和基于集成的变体，到约束形式和参数化形式。本文及配套教程（https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers）分为三个部分：第一部分介绍标准SINDy算法并逐步扩展，邀请无相关背景的读者跟随每一步并将方法适配到自己的问题中；其余两部分展示了详细的案例研究，包括（1）无人机的系统辨识和（2）混沌热虹吸换热器的辨识。通过这些示例，我们旨在证明SINDy方法实现简单，同时足够灵活，可作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具。

</details>


### [180] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在离线强化学习应用于最优治疗推荐时，使用协变量均衡诊断检测隐藏混杂/模型误设的效果。结果表明现有研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量均衡指标不足以评估此类研究，因此现有离线强化学习研究无法被判定为统计稳健。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 检测离线强化学习在治疗推荐研究中是否存在隐藏混杂或模型误设问题，评估现有研究的统计稳健性。

Method: 应用协变量均衡诊断方法来检测离线强化学习研究中的隐藏混杂或模型误设。

Result: 现有离线强化学习治疗推荐研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量均衡指标不足以评估此类研究，导致无法判定其统计稳健性。

Conclusion: 提出了未来研究方向，以获得更方法学稳健的离线强化学习在治疗推荐问题中的应用。

Original Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文探讨了在将离线强化学习应用于推导最优治疗推荐的研究中，使用协变量均衡诊断检测隐藏混杂或模型误设的效果。结果表明，要么现有的离线强化学习治疗推荐研究存在高偏倚风险，要么现有的协变量均衡指标不足以评估此类研究。无论如何，现有的离线强化学习研究无法被判定为统计稳健。结论提出了未来研究方向，以获得更方法学稳健的离线强化学习在治疗推荐问题中的应用。

</details>


### [181] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LINCS（学习在无穷小非组合草图）范畴框架，通过正切提升和余代数不动点来修复机器学习中的非组合性问题，将ML问题形式化为草图因式分解的障碍检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统机器学习依赖于损失函数的标量化和向量空间假设，缺乏对非组合性（即图无法通过商草图因式分解）的形式化处理。本文旨在建立一个严格的范畴论框架来定义、检测和修复ML中的非组合性问题。

Method: 将ML问题形式化为草图（含交换条件、极限锥、余极限余锥），定义非组合性为通用因式分解问题的失败而非算术误差。通过正切函子T提升到正切范畴，定义LINCS为Obs(Fact_S(TD))；引入INC自函子迭代正切提升形成塔结构，利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终INC余代数的存在性。

Result: 严格证明了在T_INC可被集合类实现并创造其最终载体的条件下，最终INC余代数存在。实验评估正在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习等具体ML设置中进行，详细结果见配套论文。

Conclusion: LINCS提供了一个基于范畴论的ML非组合性修复框架，将学习问题转化为余代数不动点搜索，为理解ML中的组合结构提供了新的理论视角。

Original Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一个范畴论框架——LINCS（学习在无穷小非组合草图），作为对非组合性的修复：即图无法通过提升到正切范畴设置的商草图进行因式分解的失败。机器学习问题被形式化为草图：带有交换条件D、极限锥L和余极限余簇K的图，泛化了通常的损失函数标量化或向量空间假设。非组合性被纯粹定义为通用因式分解问题的失败，而非期望预测与实际预测之间的算术误差。给定一个学习草图S=(S,D,L,K)，其底层图为S，以及一个模型D:J→C，基础缺陷是到因式分解Obs(Fact_S(D))的障碍。正切提升应用正切函子T获得TD:J→C，LINCS被定义为障碍Obs(Fact_S(TD))——询问无穷小扰动是否保持组合性约束。本文还引入了正切学习草图，即配备了Cockett-Cruttwell正切结构的草图。本文定义了INC自函子，它迭代正切提升，产生一个因式分解问题的塔D,TD,T^2D,…。因此，机器学习被形式化为对余代数不动点的搜索，其中连续的正切展开稳定（νT_INC）。利用Aczel-Mendler定理，我们证明了只要T_INC允许一个基于集合的类实现并创造其最终载体，最终INC余代数就存在。LINCS的详细实验评估正在多个具体机器学习设置中进行，包括深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习，并在配套论文中加以描述。

</details>


### [182] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Delta Signal的新型蒸馏奖励信号，用于改进强化学习中的同策略蒸馏（On-Policy Distillation）方法。Delta Signal定义为教师模型与其未经推理指令微调的基础模型之间的差异，能够更直接地传递推理能力。基于此提出的同策略Delta蒸馏（OPD²）方法在数学、科学和代码推理基准上显著优于传统同策略蒸馏，且仅需较短的训练时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有同策略蒸馏方法直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但这种方式未能充分利用教师模型在推理调优过程中产生的关键变化。作者希望找到一种更直接的信号来传递推理能力，从而减轻奖励模型的约束并提升蒸馏效率。

Method: 提出Delta Signal作为蒸馏奖励：计算教师模型（经过推理指令微调）与其基础模型（微调前）输出分布的差异，以此作为监督信号。基于此设计同策略Delta蒸馏（OPD²），在强化学习框架下使用该信号进行token级别的蒸馏。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理等多个基准测试上，OPD² consistently outperforms 传统同策略蒸馏方法，且仅需较短的训练周期就能使推理型大语言模型达到强性能。

Conclusion: Delta Signal能够有效捕获推理调优带来的变化，为推理能力迁移提供了更直接的信号。OPD²是一种高效且优于现有同策略蒸馏方法的后训练方案。

Original Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

中文摘要翻译: 同策略蒸馏是强化学习中一种替代性的后训练方法，通过从教师模型提供token级别的监督来缓解奖励模型带来的约束。尽管同策略蒸馏已在多种设置下得到研究和应用，但其基本设计仍未得到充分探索。本文引入一种新的蒸馏奖励——称为Delta信号——来替代直接模仿教师输出分布的做法。Delta信号定义为教师模型与其在推理能力指令调优之前的基础模型之间的差异。因此，它捕获了推理调优所引起的变化，并为迁移推理能力提供了更直接的信号。通过大量实证证据，我们表明Delta信号显著改善了同策略蒸馏，并将这种新的蒸馏方法称为同策略Delta蒸馏（OPD²）。在数学、科学和代码推理基准上的实验表明，OPD²持续优于传统的同策略蒸馏方法，使得推理型大语言模型仅需较短的后训练周期即可实现强性能。代码将在https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2 提供。

</details>


### [183] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，将已知ODE分量保留为显式模型，用神经网络表示未知分量，通过RTS平滑器和反向传播交替估计状态与参数，从不完整测量中学习缺失的ODE组件。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在实际应用中，许多动力系统的部分方程未知，且仅能测量到部分状态变量，传统方法难以处理这种不完整观测下的建模问题。

Method: 两阶段迭代框架：第一阶段将模型参数视为已知，使用RTS平滑器从观测数据推断潜在状态；第二阶段将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数，交替迭代直到满足预定标准。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学基准系统上进行了评估，该方法能在不完整测量条件下学习缺失的ODE分量，同时保留可解释的机械结构，并改善了潜在状态重建和长期预测性能。

Conclusion: 混合神经-物理框架能够有效结合已知物理知识与数据驱动学习，在部分观测条件下实现可解释的动力学建模，兼具准确性和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 常微分方程（ODE）被广泛用于建模物理、生物学、神经科学和生理学中的动力系统，但在许多应用中，动力学中的部分方程是未知的，且只能测量到部分状态变量。我们提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，其中ODE的已知分量保持显式，而缺失分量由神经网络表示。该方法包含两个阶段，交替进行状态估计和参数估计，并迭代直到满足预定标准。具体而言，第一步将模型参数视为已知，利用Rauch-Tung-Striebel（RTS）平滑器从可用测量数据中推断潜在状态；第二步将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。我们在涵盖线性、非线性和刚性动力学的基准系统上进行了评估，这些系统均处于部分状态观测条件下。实验结果表明，所提方法能够从不完整测量中学习缺失的ODE分量，同时利用并保留可解释的机械结构，改善了潜在状态重建和长期预测性能。

</details>


### [184] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，系统研究世界-动作模型（WAM）面临的一类新型对抗攻击——世界-动作漂移攻击，通过微小视觉扰动破坏模型想象与执行动作之间的对齐，揭示了WAM在鲁棒性和安全性方面的脆弱性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）将动作生成与未来世界预测耦合，被认为具有鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性，因为机器人的动作原则上可以对照其想象的未来进行检查。本文质疑这一假设，旨在揭示WAM在这种耦合下存在的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出BadWAM统一框架，沿攻击强度和隐蔽性两个标准刻画攻击面，实例化两类攻击：(1) 仅动作对抗攻击（直接驱动模型执行失败动作）；(2) 想象保持对抗攻击（保持模型预测的未来接近干净想象，同时诱导有害动作偏移）。

Result: 攻击显著降低了WAM在闭环执行下的任务成功率。仅动作攻击将模型性能从96.5%降至43.1%。想象保持攻击进一步揭示了WAM特有的脆弱性：适度的未来保持正则化能在保持强大攻击性能的同时减少未来想象漂移。

Conclusion: WAM的耦合特性并非天然鲁棒，存在世界-动作漂移攻击这一安全漏洞，攻击者可通过微小视觉扰动破坏想象与执行的对齐，且存在从显式动作劫持到隐蔽想象保持攻击的连续攻击谱系。

Original Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

中文摘要翻译: 世界-动作模型（World-action models, WAMs）正成为具身控制领域一种有前景的基础模型：它们不仅预测动作，还学习将动作生成与未来世界预测相耦合的表征。这种耦合通常被视为鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性的来源，因为原则上可以通过机器人想象出的未来来校验其动作。在本文中，我们证明这一假设是脆弱的。我们提出BadWAM，一个用于建模和评估世界-动作漂移攻击（World-Action Drift Attacks）的统一框架：这是一类新的、专属于WAM的对抗攻击，利用微小的视觉扰动来破坏WAM想象的内容与其执行的动作之间的对齐。BadWAM沿两个自然标准——攻击强度和隐蔽性——刻画了这一攻击面。当攻击者优先考虑破坏性时，BadWAM实例化一种仅动作对抗攻击，直接驱使模型执行导致任务失败的动作。当攻击者额外优先考虑隐蔽性时，BadWAM实例化一种想象保持对抗攻击，旨在诱导有害的动作偏移，同时使模型预测的未来保持接近其干净的想象。这两种攻击共同捕捉了WAM特有的一类失效谱系：从显式的动作劫持到更隐蔽的情况——模型看似想象了一个合理的未来，但执行了与想象不同步的动作。我们在不同WAM变体上评估了BadWAM。结果表明，我们的攻击在闭环执行下显著降低了任务成功率。例如，我们的仅动作攻击将模型性能从96.5%的成功率降至43.1%。想象保持攻击的结果进一步暴露了WAM特有的脆弱性：适度的未来保持正则化可以在保持强大攻击性能的同时减少未来想象漂移。

</details>


### [185] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多台独立同构机器的分组替换维护问题，在寿命分布未知的条件下，提出了基于多臂老虎机的数据驱动算法来学习最优替换周期，实现了理论最优的遗憾界，并验证了算法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的分组替换策略依赖已知的寿命分布来确定最优替换间隔，但在实际运维中，机器寿命分布往往是未知的，需要从含有删失的运行数据中在线学习最优策略。

Method: 将分组替换周期选择问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，提出了基于Hoeffding和Bernstein不等式的置信下界（LCB）算法；利用分组替换中嵌套观测的特殊性质，设计了相关性变体算法；同时提出了基于Kaplan-Meier估计的非参数更新算法来估计寿命分布。

Result: 所提算法达到O(K log T)遗憾界，匹配Lai-Robbins下界；相关性变体算法达到O((K-k*)log T)遗憾界，且对次优臂k<k*仅需O(1)次直接拉动；Kaplan-Meier更新算法实现几乎必然策略一致性，长期近似零遗憾。数值实验验证了理论排序，并揭示了最优分组替换与年龄相关替换之间的结构性成本差异。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实验两方面证明，所提出的数据驱动算法能够在未知寿命分布下有效学习分组替换的最优周期，且具有最优的遗憾界和良好的实际性能。

Original Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

中文摘要翻译: 我们开发了用于维护N台独立同构机器的数据驱动算法，采用分组替换策略：每台机器故障时单独更换，所有机器每隔固定长度k同时更换。目标是在寿命分布未知的情况下，从运行数据中学习成本最小化的替换间隔k*。在每个决策时刻，操作员选择k∈{1,2,...,K}，观察由此产生的故障历史（包含完整寿命和右删失寿命的混合数据），并承担由更新函数决定的单位时间成本。我们将此问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，提出了基于Hoeffding和Bernstein不等式的置信下界算法，实现了O(K log T)的遗憾界，匹配Lai-Robbins下界。利用分组替换特有的嵌套观测性质，相关性变种算法达到O((K-k*)log T)的遗憾界，且对次优臂k<k*仅需O(1)次直接拉动。此外，我们提出了一种基于Kaplan-Meier的更新算法，通过删失数据非参数估计寿命分布，实现了几乎必然的策略一致性，并在长时间尺度上达到经验近似零遗憾。我们还分析了两种平均成本马尔可夫决策过程：时间流逝模型证明了分组替换在其策略类中对任意寿命分布都是最优的；年龄向量模型在递增失效率分布下证明了单调阈值结构，并提供了黄金标准的成本基准。数值实验验证了理论排序，并揭示了最优分组替换与年龄相关替换之间的结构性成本差距。

</details>


### [186] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketch），利用KP-tree实现用户嵌入的在线更新，消除两阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧问题，显著降低数据读取需求并提升实时推荐能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 两阶段推荐中，用户嵌入在两次重训之间保持固定（嵌入陈旧），导致无法及时反映用户新行为，影响推荐实时性与准确性。

Method: 将用户偏好存储为KP-tree（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树），仅需拟合一次低秩投影，当评分到达时在线重新计算嵌入，无需模型重训练。

Result: 理论证明每次新观测值单调收紧预测误差包络（定理1）；在KuaiRec上，可变草图仅读取1.8%数据即达到0.810 RMSE（ALS读取100%数据为0.822），每批更新快8倍；新用户首次评分后<1 ms获得个性化推荐；KP-tree的范数比例采样在稀疏数据上覆盖度提升40-130%。

Conclusion: 可变草图有效解决了嵌入陈旧问题，以极低数据读取代价实现更优或相当的推荐精度，并支持毫秒级实时个性化推荐。

Original Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

中文摘要翻译: 两阶段推荐中的一个常见瓶颈是嵌入陈旧问题：当用户对某个新项目进行评分时，其嵌入向量在下一次重训周期之前保持不变。本文提出可变草图（Mutable Sketch），将每个用户的偏好存储在一棵KP-tree（一种带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）中，仅需拟合一次低秩投影，并在评分到达时在线重新计算嵌入。我们证明，每次新的观测值都会单调收紧预测误差的包络（定理1），这是FunkSVD和eALS所不具备的保证。在KuaiRec数据集上，可变草图仅读取1.8%的数据即达到0.810的RMSE，而ALS读取100%数据时为0.822，且每批更新速度快8倍。新用户在首次评分后即可在<1毫秒内获得个性化推荐，无需任何模型重训练。对不同密度区间的采样策略比较表明，在稀疏数据（密度<1%）上，KP-tree的范数比例采样可提供40-130%更好的项目覆盖率，而均匀采样在稠密矩阵上足够有效。

</details>


### [187] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体推文情感分类的方法，利用XGBoost和SHAP解释性分析来解析比特币市场情绪，而非预测价格，取得了约0.84的F1-score。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具日益普及，需要深入理解其市场行为，但现有研究多聚焦于价格预测而非市场情绪解释，因此本文旨在通过多源数据融合来阐释市场情绪。

Method: 将区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情感分类进行融合，通过归一化处理构建数据集；采用多种机器学习模型进行交叉验证，其中Gradient Boosting（XGBoost）表现最佳；使用基于博弈论的SHAP方法量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献，提升模型可解释性。

Result: XGBoost在情感分类任务中取得了约0.84的平均F1-score，证明了链上、金融与社交媒体数据的组合能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞察。

Conclusion: 多源数据组合能为加密货币情感分析提供有效的预测信号和洞见，为数据驱动的加密货币分析提供了支撑，并指出未来可通过深度学习进一步改进。

Original Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

中文摘要翻译: 随着比特币作为一种去中心化数字资产和投资工具的日益普及，人们对其市场行为的理解产生了浓厚兴趣。本研究提出了一种新方法，通过结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体帖子来分析比特币市场情绪。与旨在预测价格的不同，本工作专注于使用区块链交易、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情感分类来解释市场情绪。该方法将情感趋势与链上和金融指标融合，经过归一化处理后构建数据集，用于详细的市稠分析。通过交叉验证测试了多种机器学习模型，其中梯度提升（XGBoost）成为情感分类最可靠的模型，平均F1-score约为0.84。采用基于博弈论的模型可解释性方法SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）来量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献，提升了透明度。结果表明，这种数据组合能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞察，为数据驱动的加密货币分析提供了支撑，并支持未来通过深度学习进行改进。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [OvAi Focus: AI-based Multi-class Segmentation of Functional Ovaries and Adnexal Masses in Gynecological Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14179)
*Niccolò Tallone,Francesca Salis,Pio Raffaele Fina,Roberta Massobrio,Rosilari Bellacosa Marotti,Daniele Conti,Luca Fuso,Luca Mariani,Annamaria Ferrero,Alessandro Arena,Stefano Cosma,Dan Grisaru,Angelo Lacalandra,Renato Seracchioli,Marianna Roccio,Federica Gerace*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款名为OvAi Focus的AI医疗软件，用于超声图像中卵巢附件肿块和功能性卵巢的多类语义分割，在多个心数据集上取得了与现有方法相当或更优的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卵巢癌是致死率最高的妇科恶性肿瘤，超声图像中附件肿块和功能性卵巢的准确客观分割因操作者差异和形态复杂性而面临挑战，亟需自动化、标准化的辅助工具。

Method: 提出了一种名为OvAi Focus的独立AI软件医疗设备，对功能性卵巢和附件肿块进行多类语义分割，区分囊性成分和实性成分；在来自意大利和以色列6个中心的1,081名成年女性多中心数据集上训练并独立验证。

Result: 分割DICE得分分别为：完整病灶0.87、囊性成分0.85、实性成分0.68、功能性卵巢0.62，在多种采集设置下与最先进方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: OvAi Focus在异质性超声采集条件下实现了与最先进方法相当或更优的分割性能，有望辅助临床医生提高卵巢病变评估的准确性和客观性。

Original Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the deadliest gynecological malignancy; accurate and objective segmentation of adnexal masses and functional ovaries in ultrasound (US) remains challenging due to operator variability and morphological complexity. We present OvAi Focus (SynDiag s.r.l., Italy), a stand-alone AI software medical device that performs multi-class semantic segmentation of functional ovaries and adnexal masses, distinguishing cystic from solid components. The system was trained and independently validated on a multicenter dataset of 1,081 adult women from 6 centers across Italy and Israel. Segmentation achieved DICE scores of 0.87 (complete lesion), 0.85 (cystic), 0.68 (solid), and 0.62 (functional ovary), in line with or superior to state-of-the-art approaches across heterogeneous acquisition settings.

中文摘要翻译: 卵巢癌是致死率最高的妇科恶性肿瘤；由于操作者差异和形态复杂性，超声图像中附件肿块和功能性卵巢的准确客观分割仍然具有挑战性。我们提出了OvAi Focus（意大利SynDiag s.r.l.），一款独立的人工智能软件医疗设备，可对功能性卵巢和附件肿块进行多类语义分割，区分囊性成分和实性成分。该系统在来自意大利和以色列6个中心的1,081名成年女性多中心数据集上进行了训练和独立验证。分割结果的DICE得分分别为：完整病灶0.87、囊性成分0.85、实性成分0.68、功能性卵巢0.62，在多种异质性采集设置下与最先进方法相当或更优。

</details>


### [189] [A Hybrid Framework for Blood Vessel Morphology Classification: Discrete Geometry-based Tortuosity Feature Measurement, Information Gain-based Feature Selection, and Random Forest Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14195)
*Yu Zhong,Jingzhi Guo,Luyao Li,Zehao Wang,Zhihui Yang,Yixin Lin,Weilun Fu,Yang Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一个结合离散几何特征测量、信息增益特征选择和随机森林分类的数学框架，用于颈内动脉C1段的形态学分类与客观量化，在二元和三元分类任务中分别达到0.9206和0.8626的Macro-F1分数，并定义了形态学风险指数（MRI）以辅助临床评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 主观的血管迂曲度视觉分级高度依赖临床经验，而传统基于距离的指标难以充分表征三维空间形变。异常颈内动脉形态与脑血管评估和中风风险评价相关，因此需要客观、可重复的血管迂曲度量化方法。

Method: 提出一个数学框架，包括：(1) 从379条临床血管中心线中提取13个迂曲度特征；(2) 基于信息增益进行特征选择，降为6个特征子集（TI, AC, TC, AC/AT, AT, TT）；(3) 使用随机森林分类器进行二元（非严重/严重迂曲）和三元（直型/低迂曲/高迂曲）形态学分级。

Result: 二元分类Macro-F1分数为0.9206，三元分类Macro-F1分数为0.8626。拉伸和曲率相关特征对基础筛查具有强判别力，扭转相关特征为更详细的形态学分类提供额外信息。

Conclusion: 基于随机森林特征重要性定义了形态学风险指数（MRI），该指数可为血管形态学提供直接的数值参考，有助于实现更客观、一致的临床评估。

Original Abstract: Subjective visual grading of blood vessel tortuosity relies heavily on clinical experience, while traditional distance-based indices often fail to adequately characterize three-dimensional spatial deformation. Because abnormal internal carotid artery morphology may be clinically relevant to cerebrovascular assessment and stroke-risk evaluation, objective and reproducible quantification of vascular tortuosity is of considerable importance. To address this limitation, we propose a mathematical framework for the morphological classification of the internal carotid artery (ICA-C1) segment. The framework integrates discrete geometric feature measurement, Information Gain-based feature selection, and Random Forest classification. An initial set of 13 tortuosity features is extracted from the corresponding 379 clinical vascular centerlines using discrete geometric methods and subsequently reduced to a six-feature subset consisting of $\mathcal{TI}$, $\mathcal{AC}$, $\mathcal{TC}$, $\mathcal{AC}/\mathcal{AT}$, $\mathcal{AT}$, and $\mathcal{TT}$. The framework is evaluated in two classification tasks. For binary classification of non-severe and severe tortuosity, the RF model achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.9206. For ternary morphological grading into straight, low-tortuosity, and high-tortuosity groups, it achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.8626. The results indicate that elongation- and curvature-related features provide strong discriminatory information for basic screening, whereas torsion-related features contribute additional information for more detailed morphological classification. Based on the RF feature-importance values, we further define a Morphological Risk Index (MRI), which provides a direct numerical reference for vascular morphology and may facilitate more objective and consistent clinical assessment.

中文摘要翻译: 主观的血管迂曲度视觉分级高度依赖临床经验，而传统的基于距离的指标往往难以充分表征三维空间形变。由于异常的颈内动脉形态可能与脑血管评估和中风风险评价具有临床相关性，客观且可重复的血管迂曲度量化具有重要意义。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一个用于颈内动脉C1段形态学分类的数学框架。该框架集成了离散几何特征测量、基于信息增益的特征选择以及随机森林分类。从对应的379条临床血管中心线中，通过离散几何方法提取了初始的13个迂曲度特征，随后将其降维为由$\mathcal{TI}$、$\mathcal{AC}$、$\mathcal{TC}$、$\mathcal{AC}/\mathcal{AT}$、$\mathcal{AT}$和$\mathcal{TT}$构成的六特征子集。该框架在两个分类任务中进行了评估。在非严重与严重迂曲的二元分类中，随机森林模型达到了0.9206的Macro-F1分数。在直型、低迂曲度和高迂曲度三组形态学分级中，模型取得了0.8626的Macro-F1分数。结果表明，拉伸和曲率相关特征为基础筛查提供了强判别信息，而扭转相关特征则为更精细的形态学分类贡献了额外信息。基于随机森林的特征重要性值，我们进一步定义了形态学风险指数，该指数为血管形态学提供了直接的数值参考，可能有助于实现更客观、一致的临床评估。

</details>


### [190] [FORCE-Interior: A Poisson Flow Generative Prior for Interior Tomography Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14320)
*Kang Chen,Wenjun Xia,Jianxu Wang,Mahmud Wasif Nafee,Ge Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FORCE-Interior，一种基于泊松流生成模型的内部断层重建框架，通过全视野测量约束初始化与逐像素数据一致性机制，解决截断投影导致的重建非唯一性、过度平滑和伪影问题，在严重截断场景下显著提升结构质量和感知质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有内部CT重建方法面临三大挑战：(1) 投影截断导致逆问题严重病态，直接使用传统方法会产生非唯一解、过度平滑和截断伪影；(2) 基于学习的方法在不同截断模式、ROI尺寸和噪声水平下的泛化能力不足；(3) 现有生成模型方法主要针对非内部断层场景设计，不能直接处理基于ROI的投影截断，且缺乏足够的数据一致性约束会导致生成结果在解剖上合理但与测量不一致。

Method: 提出FORCE-Interior框架，包含两个关键机制：(1) 全视野（full-FOV）测量约束初始化——利用完整视野的测量数据对生成过程进行初始化；(2) 逐像素数据一致性——在泊松流生成采样的每一步中对ROI截断测量施加数据一致性约束，确保生成过程始终锚定于实际采集的测量数据。

Result: 实验表明，FORCE-Interior在两个更严重截断的ROI尺寸上实现了更好的结构和感知重建质量，在最大ROI尺寸上也取得了有竞争力的结果，同时保持投影域的一致性。

Conclusion: FORCE-Interior通过将泊松流生成模型与针对截断测量的数据一致性约束相结合，有效解决了内部CT重建中的病态问题，在严重截断场景下优于现有方法，具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Interior tomography reconstructs a region of interest (ROI) from truncated projection measurements. However, projection truncation makes the inverse problem severely ill-posed, leading to non-unique solutions, oversmoothing, and truncation-induced artifacts when conventional reconstruction methods are directly applied to interior tomography. Existing learning-based interior CT methods have shown promising performance, but their generalization across different truncation patterns, ROI sizes, and noise levels remains an important challenge. Meanwhile, current generative model-based reconstruction methods are primarily designed for non-interior tomography settings and do not directly address ROI-based projection truncation. Moreover, without sufficient data-consistency constraints, generative sampling may yield anatomically plausible but measurement-inconsistent structures. To address these challenges, we propose FORCE-Interior, a Poisson-flow generative reconstruction framework for interior tomography. FORCE-Interior combines a full-FOV measurement-constrained initialization with per-step data consistency for ROI-truncated measurements, anchoring generative sampling to the acquired measurements throughout reconstruction. Experiments show that FORCE-Interior achieves improved structural and perceptual reconstruction quality at the two more severely truncated ROI sizes, with competitive results at the largest, while maintaining projection-domain consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 内部断层重建从截断的投影测量中重建感兴趣区域（ROI）。然而，投影截断使得逆问题严重病态，导致直接应用传统重建方法时出现非唯一解、过度平滑和截断引起的伪影。现有的基于学习的内部CT方法已展现出良好的性能，但它们在跨不同截断模式、ROI尺寸和噪声水平下的泛化能力仍然是一个重要挑战。同时，当前基于生成模型的重建方法主要针对非内部断层场景设计，不能直接处理基于ROI的投影截断问题。此外，在缺乏充分的数据一致性约束的情况下，生成采样可能产生解剖上合理但与测量不一致的结构。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了FORCE-Interior，一种用于内部断层重建的泊松流生成重建框架。FORCE-Interior将全视野测量约束初始化与针对ROI截断测量的逐像素数据一致性相结合，在整个重建过程中将生成采样锚定于采集到的测量数据。实验表明，FORCE-Interior在两个截断更严重的ROI尺寸上实现了改进的结构和感知重建质量，在最大ROI尺寸上也取得了有竞争力的结果，同时保持了投影域的一致性。

</details>


### [191] [ViPSAM: Visual Prompting Medical Image Segmentation Using Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14328)
*San Lee,Nalee Kim,Jeong Il Yu,Hee Chul Park,Boah Kim*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ViPSAM是一个基于SAM的视觉提示框架，通过引入对比增强MRI的引导信息，提升非对比CT图像中肝脏病变的分割精度，在质子治疗规划中表现出优于U-Net和SAM基线方法的效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在质子治疗规划中，呼吸门控非对比CT（NCCT）常用于病变分割，但由于病变与背景对比度低，准确分割极具挑战。受临床实践中医生参考对比增强MRI来勾画NCCT病变的启发，作者提出利用跨模态互补信息来增强非对比图像分割性能。

Method: 基于Segment Anything Model (SAM)，ViPSAM包含三个核心组件：(1) 视觉提示编码器，用于从对比增强图像中提取引导特征；(2) 视觉引导交叉注意力模块，用于融合非对比和对比增强特征，增强低对比区域的病变相关表征；(3) 参数高效的掩码解码器适配策略，有效利用视觉提示信息。

Result: 在质子治疗中采集的肝脏病变NCCT分割任务上，ViPSAM在性能上优于代表性的U-Net和SAM类方法，验证了跨模态视觉提示能够实现更鲁棒、更准确的非对比图像分割。

Conclusion: 跨模态视觉提示策略可有效利用对比增强图像的互补信息，显著提升非对比CT图像中低对比度病变的分割质量，为质子治疗规划中的自动分割提供了一种新范式。

Original Abstract: In proton therapy planning, respiratory-gated non-contrast CT (NCCT) is commonly used for lesion segmentation; however, accurate delineation remains challenging due to low lesion-to-background contrast. Although learning-based methods have shown strong performance, they often struggle with non-contrast image segmentation. Inspired by clinical practice, where contrast-enhanced MRI is referenced to delineate lesions on NCCT, we propose ViPSAM, a visual prompting framework that leverages complementary cross-modality information. Built upon the Segment Anything Model (SAM), ViPSAM introduces a visual prompt encoder to extract guidance features from contrast-enhanced images and a visual-guided cross-attention module to integrate non-contrast and contrast-enhanced features, thereby enhancing lesion-relevant representations in low-contrast regions. The mask decoder is further adapted in a parameter-efficient manner to utilize visual prompts effectively. We evaluate the proposed method on liver lesion segmentation using NCCT acquired for proton therapy. Experimental results demonstrate that ViPSAM outperforms representative U-Net- and SAM-based methods, indicating that cross-modality visual prompting enables more robust and accurate segmentation in non-contrast images.

中文摘要翻译: 在质子治疗规划中，呼吸门控非对比CT（NCCT）常用于病变分割；然而，由于病变与背景之间的对比度较低，准确勾画仍然具有挑战性。尽管基于学习的方法展现了较强的性能，但在非对比图像分割中仍然面临困难。受临床实践中医生参考对比增强MRI来勾画NCCT上病变的启发，我们提出了ViPSAM，一种利用互补跨模态信息的视觉提示框架。ViPSAM基于Segment Anything Model（SAM），引入了一个视觉提示编码器用于从对比增强图像中提取引导特征，以及一个视觉引导交叉注意力模块用于融合非对比与对比增强特征，从而增强低对比区域中与病变相关的表征。掩码解码器进一步以参数高效的方式适配，以有效利用视觉提示。我们在质子治疗中获取的肝脏病变NCCT数据上对所提方法进行了评估。实验结果表明，ViPSAM优于具有代表性的U-Net和SAM类方法，表明跨模态视觉提示能够实现更鲁棒、更准确的非对比图像分割。

</details>


### [192] [Domain Adaptation of Mismatched Proximal Denoiser for Plug-and-Play Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14894)
*Guixian Xu,Jinglai Li,Junqi Tang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出“近端失配”概念，量化去噪器在域外使用时对PnP-PGD收敛的影响，推导了以𝒪(1/K)速率衰减的平稳性界，并提出基于近端匹配的适应方法，在域偏移下显著优于传统的MSE适应方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的PnP-PGD收敛分析假设去噪器满足结构性条件（如近端映射或压缩映射），但未考虑去噪器在部署时面临的域偏移问题，缺乏对域失配如何影响收敛的理论刻画。

Method: 定义“近端失配”为部署去噪器与目标域参考近端映射之间的差异；推导了平稳性界，该界以𝒪(1/K)速率衰减并附加与平均平方近端失配成正比的项；提出基于近端匹配的适应策略，而非传统的MSE适应。

Result: 在高斯去模糊和超分辨率任务中，当发生显著域偏移时，基于近端匹配的适应方法在重建质量上显著优于MSE适应方法，尤其在少样本场景下提升最大。

Conclusion: 近端匹配适应能有效缓解域失配带来的近端失配问题，比MSE适应更适合PnP-PGD框架下的图像重建任务。

Original Abstract: Plug-and-play proximal gradient descent (PnP-PGD) enables flexible image reconstruction by using denoisers as implicit priors. In practice, these denoisers are often deployed outside their training domains. Existing analyses establish convergence under structural assumptions on the deployed denoiser, such as requiring it to be a proximal map or a contraction. However, they do not measure how domain mismatch affects convergence of PnP-PGD. We define this effect as \emph{proximal mismatch}: the discrepancy between a deployed denoiser $\widehat{\mathsf D}$ and a target-domain reference map $\mathsf D_\star=\operatorname{prox}_{R_\star}$ associated with the underlying regularizer $R_\star$. Under this mismatch, each denoising update becomes an inexact proximal step for the target objective. We further derive a stationarity bound that decays at a rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/K)$, with an additive term proportional to the average squared proximal mismatch. This result motivates adaptation via proximal matching rather than MSE-based adaptation alone. We study this approach with two established denoiser families: learned proximal networks and gradient-step denoisers. Experiments on Gaussian deblurring and super-resolution under substantial domain shift show that proximal matching adaptation improves reconstruction quality significantly over MSE-based adaptation, yielding the largest numerical gains in the few-shot regime.

中文摘要翻译: 即插即用近端梯度下降（PnP-PGD）通过将去噪器作为隐式先验实现灵活的图像重建。在实际应用中，这些去噪器往往在其训练域之外部署。现有分析在部署去噪器满足结构性假设（如要求其为近端映射或压缩映射）下建立了收敛性，但未衡量域失配对PnP-PGD收敛的影响。我们将这一效应定义为**近端失配**：部署去噪器$\widehat{\mathsf D}$与目标域参考映射$\mathsf D_\star=\operatorname{prox}_{R_\star}$（关联于底层正则化器$R_\star$）之间的差异。在此失配下，每一次去噪更新都成为目标目标的一个不精确近端步。我们进一步推导出一个平稳性界，该界以$\mathcal{O}(1/K)$速率衰减，并附加一个与平均平方近端失配成正比的项。这一结果启发我们采用基于近端匹配的适应方法，而非仅依赖基于MSE的适应。我们通过两种成熟的去噪器系列——学习型近端网络和梯度步去噪器——研究了这一方法。在显著域偏移下的高斯去模糊和超分辨率实验表明，基于近端匹配的适应方法在重建质量上显著优于基于MSE的适应方法，在少样本场景下获得了最大的数值提升。

</details>


### [193] [Deep Scene-Driven Ordering of Hadamard Basis for Single-Pixel Spectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15045)
*Brayan Monroy,Hans Garcia,Henry Arguello,Jorge Bacca*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种场景驱动的Hadamard基排序方法，用于改进单像素成像（SPI）中光谱图像的获取质量，通过端到端优化实现灵活调制模式选择，在VIS和NIR光谱图像上优于固定设计方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统SPI结合深度光学编码设计（DOCD）的方法缺乏反馈机制，光学设计非反馈式导致图像质量受限，仅在训练场景下达到最优性能，无法适应多样化场景的光谱成像需求。

Method: 提出一种场景驱动的Hadamard基排序框架，在SPI架构中根据场景特征对Hadamard矩阵进行灵活排序，通过端到端优化实现调制模式的自适应选择，替代传统的固定光学设计。

Result: 在光谱数据集仿真和真实测试平台采集实验中，所提方法在可见光（VIS）和近红外（NIR）光谱图像质量上相比固定设计方法均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 场景驱动的Hadamard基排序方法能有效提升SPI光谱成像质量，为高空间-光谱分辨率图像获取提供了一种更灵活、自适应的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Spectral images are highly valuable for various applications, including environmental monitoring and precision agriculture. However, the high cost of specialized sensors limits the wide use of this technology in numerous applications. Current alternatives to acquire high spatial-spectral resolution spectral images, like Single-Pixel Imaging (SPI) enhanced with Deep Optical Coding Design (DOCD), have limitations due to their non-feedback optical designs, leading to limited image quality, with optimal performance achieved only for the specific scenes used during training. This work reformulates the DOCD framework to handle the scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard basis within the SPI architecture for spectral imaging. Taking into account that SPI usually acquires hundreds of snapshots, our approach introduces a scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard matrix for flexible SPI modulation pattern selection based on scene characteristics in an end-to-end optimization. Simulations on spectral datasets and real test-bed acquisitions demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in improving the quality of VIS and NIR spectral images compared to fixed designs.

中文摘要翻译: 光谱图像在环境监测和精准农业等多种应用中具有极高价值。然而，专用传感器的高成本限制了该技术在众多应用中的广泛使用。当前获取高空间-光谱分辨率光谱图像的替代方案，如结合深度光学编码设计（DOCD）的单像素成像（SPI），由于其非反馈式光学设计存在局限性，导致图像质量受限，仅在训练所用的特定场景下才能达到最优性能。本工作重新构建了DOCD框架，以处理SPI架构中光谱成像的Hadamard基的场景驱动排序。考虑到SPI通常采集数百次快照，我们的方法引入了一种场景驱动的Hadamard矩阵排序，通过端到端优化，基于场景特征灵活选择SPI调制模式。在光谱数据集上的仿真和真实测试平台采集实验证明，与固定设计相比，所提方法在提升可见光和近红外光谱图像质量方面具有有效性。

</details>


### [194] [ESAR: Event-Based Synthetic Aperture Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15073)
*Harbir Antil,Daniel Blauvelt,David Sayre*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出将单目事件相机成像建模为合成孔径逆问题，通过恢复静态场景的对数辐射场而非动态潜在图像，利用正则化反演在近天底运动下重建一致的大尺度空间结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有事件相机重建方法通常重建动态潜在图像或像素-时间体，忽略了场景的静态几何结构，导致病态性较强。本文旨在利用静态场景的几何先验来改善重建质量。

Method: 将事件相机成像建模为合成孔径逆问题，引入静态场景对数辐射场 $\theta$，通过几何映射 $P$ 将静态场景映射为运动依赖的潜在视图，并结合时间差分算子 $A$ 得到线性化模型 $AP\theta = b + \eta$，最后采用正则化反演恢复 $\theta$。

Result: 在模拟数据和真实Falcon Neuro近天底事件数据上的实验表明，所提基于 $\theta$ 的公式相比动态潜在图像基线和学习式事件重建基线，能恢复更连贯的大尺度空间结构，同时抑制细尺度纹理。

Conclusion: 基于静态场景对数辐射场的正则化反演方法能有效利用合成孔径结构，在近天底运动下实现相干的大尺度结构重建，优于传统动态重建方法。

Original Abstract: Event cameras report asynchronous polarity events when changes in log--radiance exceed a fixed contrast threshold, producing signed temporal contrast measurements rather than conventional image frames. We formulate monocular event-based imaging as a synthetic-aperture inverse problem for a static ground-domain log--radiance field $θ\in \mathbb{R}^{N_g}$. Instead of reconstructing a latent pixel-time volume $v \in \mathbb{R}^{N_pN_t}$, we impose the geometric relation $v=Pθ$, where $P$ maps the fixed scene into motion-dependent latent views. Aggregating events over finite time intervals gives the linearized model \[
  APθ= b+η, \] where $A$ is a temporal differencing operator, $b$ contains signed binned event counts, and $η$ represents measurement and modeling errors. This decomposition exposes a synthetic-aperture structure: under near-nadir motion, successive projections are approximately shifted views of a common scene, while the composite operator $AP$ remains ill-conditioned because it combines spatial averaging with temporal differencing. We therefore use regularized inversion to recover $θ$. Numerical experiments on simulated data and real near-nadir Falcon Neuro event data show that the proposed $θ$-based formulation recovers coherent large-scale spatial structure, relative to dynamic latent-image and learned event-reconstruction baselines, while suppressing fine-scale texture.

中文摘要翻译: 事件相机在日志辐射度变化超过固定对比度阈值时报告异步极性事件，产生带符号的时间对比度测量，而非传统图像帧。本文将单目事件成像建模为针对静态场景域对数辐射场 $\theta\in \mathbb{R}^{N_g}$ 的合成孔径逆问题。不同于重建潜在像素-时间体 $v \in \mathbb{R}^{N_pN_t}$，我们施加几何关系 $v=P\theta$，其中 $P$ 将固定场景映射为运动依赖的潜在视图。在有限时间区间上聚合事件得到线性化模型 \[ AP\theta = b + \eta, \] 其中 $A$ 是时间差分算子，$b$ 包含带符号的事件分箱计数，$\eta$ 代表测量和建模误差。该分解揭示了合成孔径结构：在近天底运动下，连续投影近似为公共场景的平移视图，而复合算子 $AP$ 由于结合了空间平均与时间差分仍然是病态的。因此，我们采用正则化反演来恢复 $\theta$。在模拟数据和真实近天底Falcon Neuro事件数据上的数值实验表明，与动态潜在图像和学习式事件重建基线相比，所提出的基于 $\theta$ 的公式在抑制细尺度纹理的同时，恢复了连贯的大尺度空间结构。

</details>
